agot_spawn_canon_cradle_dragon_effect = {
	$CRADLEMATE$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_13_b }
		}
		agot_spawn_anogaros_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_13_d }
		}
		agot_spawn_archonei_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Belaerys_1 }
		}
		agot_spawn_terrax_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_21 }
		}
		agot_spawn_meraxes_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_30 }
		}
		agot_spawn_quicksilver_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_35 }
		}
		agot_spawn_vermithor_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_36 }
		}
		agot_spawn_silverwing_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Velaryon_35 }
		}
		agot_spawn_seasmoke_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_75 }
		}
		agot_spawn_vermax_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_76 }
		}
		agot_spawn_arrax_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_77 }
		}
		agot_spawn_tyraxes_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_78 }
		}
		agot_spawn_stormcloud_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_69 }
		}
		agot_spawn_moondancer_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_70 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = hatched_wyrmling }
			agot_spawn_morning_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			agot_spawn_rhaena_1_effect = yes
			scope:dragon = {
				trigger_event = { id = agot_dragon.9000 days = 1 }
			}
			remove_character_flag = hatched_canon_dragon
			add_character_flag = hatched_wyrmling
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_72 }
		}
		agot_spawn_shrykos_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_71 }
		}
		agot_spawn_morghul_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Velaryon_44 }
		}
		agot_spawn_laena_1_effect = yes
		#needs murder events
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_74 }
		}
		agot_spawn_nightfyre_effect = yes
		#needs murder events
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:owner = { has_character_flag = is_Targaryen_9 }
		}
		agot_spawn_drogon_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		create_character = {
			location = scope:owner.location
			template = agot_dragon_hatchling_template
			random_traits = no
			save_scope_as = dragon
		}
	}
}

agot_set_reserved_canon_egg_scope = {
	$EGG$ = { save_scope_as = egg }
	if = {
		limit = { agot_is_valid_vhagar_birth = { EGG = scope:egg } }
		save_scope_as = vhagar_birth_reserved
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_is_valid_serporoth_birth = { EGG = scope:egg } }
		save_scope_as = serporoth_birth_reserved

	random_list = {
		1 = { agot_spawn_nagga_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_urrax_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_5_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_1_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_6_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_2_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_vassarion_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_essovius_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_valryon_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ghiscar_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_vermithrax_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_balerion_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_nessarion_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_3_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_3_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_rovax_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_ancestor_4_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_neltharion_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_pythax_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_rhaegal_effect = yes }
		1 = { agot_spawn_viserion_effect = yes }
	}
}

agot_spawn_nagga_effect = {  # (History id = dragon_nagga)
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = canon_gender
		value = flag:female
	}
	create_character = {
		name = "Nagga"
		gender_female_chance = dragon_female_chance
		culture = culture:dragon_culture
		faith = faith:valyrian_pan_freehold
		location = scope:owner.location
		age = 0
		#mother = character:UNKNOWN
		dynasty = none
		trait = dragon
		trait = dragon_physique_good_3

		random_traits = no
		save_scope_as = dragon

		# Nagga
		after_creation = {
			make_trait_inactive = is_dragon_nagga

			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_gender_hidden
				value = 1
			}

			create_story = story_dragon_alive

			# Needed for visuals
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_age
				value = age
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_size
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_size_base
				value = 0
			}

			agot_back_apply_dragon_aging_effect = yes

			# Dragon Appearance
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_primary_color_hue
				value = 0.54
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_fire_color_template
				value = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_fire_color_value
				value = 1.0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_fire_smoke_template
				value = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_fire_smoke_value
				value = 1.0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_metallic_scales_strength
				value = 0.6
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_scale_shadows
				value = 0.5
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_scale_highlights
				value = 0.5
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_wounded
				value = 0.12
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_body_shading_template
				value = 7
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_body_shading_value
				value = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_body_pattern_template
				value = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_body_pattern_value
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_wings_shading_template
				value = 7
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_wings_shading_value
				value = 0.85
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_primary_color_value
				value = 0.482
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_primary_color_saturation
				value = 0.42
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_secondary_hue
				value = 0.58
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_tertiary_hue
				value = 0.56
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_tertiary_value
				value = 0.61
			}

				name = primary_color
				value = flag:green
			}

			agot_dragon_transfer_vars_to_story_cycle_effect = { DRAGON = scope:dragon }

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_5

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0002 # dragon_aggressive
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0006 # dragon_defiant
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0008 # dragon_imperious
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
agot_spawn_essovius_effect = {  # (History id = dragon_essovius)
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = canon_gender
		value = flag:male
	}
	create_character = {
		name = "Essovius"
		gender_female_chance = dragon_female_chance
		culture = culture:dragon_culture

		trait = dragon

		# Essovius
		after_creation = {
			make_trait_inactive = is_dragon_essovius

				name = dragon_gender_hidden
				value = 1

				value = flag:middle
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = primary_color
				value = flag:white
			}

			agot_dragon_transfer_vars_to_story_cycle_effect = { DRAGON = scope:dragon }

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_3

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0011 # dragon_cooperative
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0001 # dragon_friendly
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0013 # dragon_restrained
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}

				value = 0.17

				name = primary_color_grading
				value = flag:darker
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = primary_color
				value = flag:grey
			}

			agot_dragon_transfer_vars_to_story_cycle_effect = { DRAGON = scope:dragon }

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_2

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0002 # dragon_aggressive
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0010 # dragon_impulsive
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0004 # dragon_bloodthirsty
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
agot_spawn_ancestor_4_effect = { # (History id = dragon_ancestor_4)
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = canon_gender
		value = flag:female
	}
	create_character = {
		name = "Peldion"
		gender_female_chance = dragon_female_chance
		culture = culture:dragon_culture
		faith = faith:valyrian_pan_freehold
		location = scope:owner.location
		age = 0
		#mother = character:UNKNOWN
		dynasty = none
		trait = dragon

		random_traits = no
		save_scope_as = dragon

		#Peldion
		after_creation = {
			make_trait_inactive = is_dragon_ancestor_4

			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_gender_hidden
				value = 1
			}

			create_story = story_dragon_alive

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits

					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0001 # dragon_friendly

				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0013 # dragon_restrained
					years = 6
				}

					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0003 # dragon_skittish
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}

					years = 15
				}

				#No canon traits
			}
		}
	}

agot_spawn_dreamfyre_effect = {  # (History id = dragon_dreamfyre)
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = canon_gender
		value = flag:female
	}
	create_character = {
		name = "Dreamfyre"
		gender_female_chance = dragon_female_chance
		culture = culture:dragon_culture
		faith = faith:valyrian_pan_freehold
		location = scope:owner.location
		age = 0
		mother = character:dragon_vhagar
		dynasty = none
		trait = dragon

		random_traits = no
		save_scope_as = dragon

		# Dreamfyre
		after_creation = {
			make_trait_inactive = is_dragon_dreamfyre

			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_gender_hidden
				value = 1
			}

			create_story = story_dragon_alive

				}

					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}

			set_variable = dragon_is_fertile

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits

					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0005 # dragon_accepting

				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0011 # dragon_cooperative
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0001 # dragon_friendly
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				name = gene_dragon_cheek_width
				value = 0.4
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_head_roundness
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_brow_width
				value = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_outer_brow_height
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = primary_color_grading
				value = flag:middle
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = primary_color
				value = flag:red
			}

			agot_dragon_transfer_vars_to_story_cycle_effect = { DRAGON = scope:dragon }

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_3

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0008 # dragon_imperious
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0005 # dragon_accepting
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0013 # dragon_restrained
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				#No canon traits
			}

agot_spawn_aerea_3_effect = { # (History id = dragon_aerea_3)
	save_scope_value_as = {

	}

		name = "Adereon"

				name = gene_dragon_wings_shading_value

				name = primary_color_grading
				value = flag:middle
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = primary_color
				value = flag:grey
			}

			agot_dragon_transfer_vars_to_story_cycle_effect = { DRAGON = scope:dragon }

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_3

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0007 # dragon_supporting
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0009 # dragon_calculating
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0012 # dragon_solitary
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits

				}

					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0012 # dragon_solitary
					years = 10
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9999
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}

					years = 15
				}

		age = 0

		trait = dragon_ugly

			#Stop current egg from being reforged
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:egg
				}
				scope:egg = {
					set_variable = { name = currently_hatching }
				}
			}
			agot_dragon_birth_magic_effect = yes

			#Canon Traits
			if = {
				limit = { has_game_rule = agot_canon_dragons_enabled_traits }

				add_character_flag = agot_canon_education_dragon_2

				add_character_flag = agot_developing_canon_dragon_traits
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0006 # dragon_defiant
					years = 3
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.0010 # dragon_impulsive
					years = 6
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = agot_dragon_canon_personality.9998
					years = 7
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
agot_spawn_viserion_effect = {  # (History id = dragon_viserion)
	save_scope_value_as = {
		name = canon_gender
		value = flag:male
	}
	create_character = {
		name = "Viserion"
		gender_female_chance = dragon_female_chance
		culture = culture:dragon_culture
		faith = faith:valyrian_pan_freehold
		location = scope:owner.location
		age = 0
		mother = character:dragon_dreamfyre
		dynasty = none
		trait = dragon
		trait = dragon_destined
		trait = dragon_physique_good_2

		random_traits = no
		save_scope_as = dragon

		# Viserion
		after_creation = {
			make_trait_inactive = is_dragon_viserion

			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_gender_hidden
				value = 1
			}

			create_story = story_dragon_alive

			# Needed for visuals
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_age
				value = age
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_size
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = dragon_size_base
				value = 0
			}

			agot_back_apply_dragon_aging_effect = yes

			# Dragon Appearance
			set_variable = {
				name = gene_dragon_primary_color_value
				value = 0.554
			}
